# Chinese 03 — Band 3-oriented independent communication

`deck:zh-Hans-CN:03` contains 800 original reviewed production cards,
`zh-03-0001`–`zh-03-0800`, with one ordered mapping each. Together with accepted
Chinese 00–02's 1,200 cards/maps, delivered Chinese text totals 2,000. All new rows
are P: standard spoken Chinese (Putonghua), ordinary Simplified production.
The [pinned guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#64-independent-communication),
[Chinese plan #3](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/3#stage-03) and
[execution #31](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/31) define this scope.
Band orientation is a curriculum grouping; it does not establish a new examination
syllabus, CEFR equivalence or attained proficiency.

| Block | Actual counters | Cards | Main work |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| A | 0001–0130 | 130 | Common results, literal and extended direction, lexical hosts and viewpoint |
| B | 0131–0245 | 115 | Affected objects, passive agency, potential and practical feasibility |
| C | 0246–0355 | 110 | Modification, topics, reference and recoverable omission |
| D | 0356–0460 | 105 | Rhetorical questions, final particles, stance and responses |
| E | 0461–0560 | 100 | Conditions, concessions, causes, purposes and consequences |
| F | 0561–0680 | 120 | Register alternatives, discourse and narrative/explanatory collocations |
| G | 0681–0800 | 120 | Word senses, compounds and interactional repair |

## Required families and important contrasts

All counters below use `zh-03-`. Ranges locate work; the individual mapping names
the actual hidden objective. Supporting visible grammar receives no second hidden
credit, and lexical counterparts do not become separate grammar concepts.

| Family | Actual hidden retrieval and contrasts |
| --- | --- |
| Common results | 0001–0040 develop attained skills, loss, exhaustion, physical and cognitive changes with actual hosts. 0081–0120 add restoration, perception, capacity, arrest, discrimination, transformation, alignment, drying, dilution, magnification and lexical result combinations. Compare cognitive 透0002 with through-wetness0103, surface 平0024 with crushing 扁0104, and physical 顺0099 with event ordering0113. |
| Literal and extended direction | 0041–0070 distinguish onset, recall, storage, aggregation, assessment, preservation, continuation, recognition, production, recovery, passage through difficulty and contact. 0071–0080 retain actual motion/lexical-host and observer relations; 0121–0122 retrieve deictic 来/去 with intervening objects/place goals. 0123–0129 extend return, answer production, swallowing, acceptance and mention. Physical direction is compared with actual accepted 02 forms; these are not noun substitutions. |
| 把 and affected objects | 0131–0145 broaden final location, recipient, transformation, clausal result, count/duration, brief action, nonintentional causes and modal/intention/near-miss scope. 0142把手 is honestly lexical; it is not another 把 construction. |
| Passive and agency | 0146–0159 distinguish overt/unknown actors, beneficial0148 and nonhuman0149 events, negated/pending/future/ongoing/repeated events, and conversational 让/叫/给. 0160–0165 extend undergoing, responsibility, substitution, causation and beneficiary roles. No blanket adversity rule. |
| Potential and event status | 0166–0200 cover perception, comprehension, acquisition, retention/recall, strength, time, capacity, affordability, availability, timeliness, tolerance, sleep, reach and paths. 0201–0210 add stability, resistance, usefulness, attention, workload, scheduling, compatibility, agreement and lexical evaluations. 0211–0220 distinguish capacity, eligibility, permission, prohibition, failed past achievement and feasibility; attaining a result is not merely being able to attain it. 0221–0245 extend turns, delegation, ownership, repair, damage and prevention with actual participants. |
| Complex modification | 0246–0252 retrieve headed object/place/time relatives, partitive membership, instrumental function and content clauses. 0253–0280 extend identifying heads, bounded sets, anaphoric modifiers, pending/imminent status, affiliation and correspondence. A new noun or shorter lexical form is labeled lexical where appropriate. |
| Topic, information and reference | 0281–0294 establish topics, viewpoints, contrast, scalar focus and quantity bounds. 0295–0324 distinguish new/given participants, proposition/manner reference, whole/subset/remainder, reciprocal/reflexive reference, delegation and temporal/place reference. 0325–0355 include omission, reference repair, quotation/source scope, bounded generalization and natural elliptical replies with recoverable content. |
| Rhetorical questions | 0356–0375 make the challenge, rejected claim, shared fact, practical alternative or response implication recoverable. Genuine information questions remain distinct. |
| Final particles and responses | 0376–0405 distinguish reluctant acceptance, softened direction, dilemma/concession, affirmation, degree, challenge/alternatives, commitment, dismissal, shared knowledge, complaint, coaxing, resignation, warning/appeal, linked particle forms, change, restriction and settled advice. Neutral readings follow actual functions; imagined intonation cannot supply the stance. |
| Stance and evidence | 0406–0460 separate surprise, confirmation, explanation, evaluation, insistence, uncertainty, inference, attributed opinion, trust, doubt, confidence, acknowledgment/rejection, correction of assumptions, commitment, relief and regret. Evidence and limits stay inside each Chinese scene. |
| Conditions and concessions | 0461–0480 distinguish hypothetical assumptions, contingencies, trigger conditions, necessary exceptions, alternatives, locally sufficient actions, prerequisites and necessary/sufficient conditions. 0481–0495 contrast hypothetical and established concessions, degree/manner extremes and unconditional alternatives. 0496–0500 add actual action preferences. Established-premise 既然 is prior hidden retrieval at zh-02-0397; 0508如此 adds situation-anaphoric retrieval within that known frame, not another new premise construction. |
| Cause, consequence and purpose | 0501–0520 cover causal links, evidence-based inference, motives, counterfactual otherwise, causal relation versus mere connection, grounds and justification. 0521–0540 distinguish intended purposes, preventive preparation, enabling another actor, resource use and goal/intention nouns. 0541–0560 add reasons, actual outcomes, follow-up, prompting/constraint, helpful/favorable/adverse/no-help relations, counterexpectation and continuing/future consequences. Purposes are not asserted achievements; no help0554 is not harm0553. |
| Register and discourse | 0561–0600 retrieve register-sensitive lexical alternatives with actual recipients, occasions and time/status distinctions. 0601–0618 organize reasons, aspects, addition, sequence, gradual change, intervals/processes, examples, detail, role reversal and comparison. 0619–0640 include faithful summaries, evidence/source limits, contrast, scalar focus, approximation and event order. 0641–0676 develop natural practical collocations with explicit participants, effects and bounded abilities; 0677–0680 distinguish recording, a stated inquiry method, complete-set tallying and a numerator/denominator proportion. New forms beside known counterparts remain lexical credits. |
| Expanded senses and repair | 0681–0720 retrieve contextual polyphones and distinct noun, verb and modifier senses, including bound repetition, quantity nouns, numerical symbols and differences, leaving behind versus falling, music, reaction, and salary context. 0721–0760 add everyday exchange, independence, limits, duties, payments, reductions, change, reimbursement and practical repairs. 0761–0800 distinguish clarification, mishearing, calculation, qualification, transmission, interruption, relevant response, viewpoint, quantity and time repair, explicit stance and matching request formulations. 0691种子 is first focused retrieval after visible support at zh-02-0270, not first exposure. |

## Progression and situation breadth

The prerequisite cells identify actual earlier hidden retrieval or explicitly
introduced visible support. Earlier IDs retain their accepted meanings; references
are not inferred from target strings. New lexical forms, senses and collocations
remain legitimate learning without multiplying generic concept credits. Repeated
answers occur when their functions or combinations differ; no global uniqueness
quota enlarges the blanks. Focused components retain their host, participants,
result, reference and stance in the Chinese front. Source summaries contain short
original evidence, attribution and appropriately limited conclusions.

Contexts include household tasks, food, movement, purchases, neighbors, family and
social arrangements, study and practical work, original narration, small records,
opinions and repair. Later work includes growing seeds, rain, music, borrowed household items, activity-room duties, paper purchases, a returned expense, meeting messages, a soup offer and tactful requests. Work and study share space with home, neighborhood and ordinary social interaction. They are fictional local situations,
not copied source sentences, real-person quotations or external research claims.
No advanced literary/idiomatic survey or Chinese 04 production is included.

## Descriptive lexical and character measures

| Collection | Cards | Exact targets | Exact lemmas | Operational lemma-meaning pairs | Direct-target Han | Sentence Han | Visible-context Han | Incidental-only Han |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Accepted 00–02 | 1200 | 1104 | 1104 | 1200 | 920 | 1026 | 750 | 106 |
| New 03 | 800 | 740 | 740 | 800 | 576 | 1165 | 1030 | 589 |
| Cumulative 00–03 | 2000 | 1765 | 1765 | 1999 | 1103 | 1336 | 1112 | 233 |

Exact targets and lemmas are sets of their NFC source strings; the operational
lemma-meaning measure counts distinct ordered pairs of those exact fields.
Units include words, compact phrases and grammatical components. Gloss-string
differences alone do not prove distinct senses, and these are not dictionary word
counts or a semantic tokenizer. New 03 contributes 661 target strings, 661 lemma strings and 799 operational lemma-meaning pairs absent from 00–02. The exact 差点 / “almost, but did not” pair repeats zh-02-0047 at 03-0140: the earlier clock scene establishes a narrowly avoided event; 0140 explicitly develops its scope over 把电脑弄湿, with the affected-object result averted. It is one repeated lexical pair and a distinct combination, not another newly learned generic near-miss meaning.

Han sets use Unicode CJK UNIFIED IDEOGRAPH and CJK COMPATIBILITY IDEOGRAPH code
points in clozes and sentences, including proper names. Direct-target Han occur
in a cloze. Visible-context Han occur after removing that card's single cloze;
they can overlap direct targets elsewhere. Incidental-only Han are sentence Han
minus all direct-target Han within the measured collection. Notes, English,
Pinyin, mappings, punctuation and Arabic digits are excluded. Han numeral
characters are counted as Han. Exposure does not show
recognition or handwriting. The guide's approximately 900 recognized characters,
2,245 words/phrases and 300 handwritten characters are separate landmarks,
not card quotas, caps or claims of attainment. This is the assigned 2,000-card
progression report, not the final ten-stage/10,000-card reconciliation.

## Reading and review limits

Every card has tone-marked Pinyin for its whole hidden span, contextual polyphones,
word boundaries and actual neutral syllables. Lexical tones are retained, with
useful connected-speech changes labeled separately. The scoped choices use
[GB/T16159–2012](https://www.moe.gov.cn/ewebeditor/uploadfile/2015/01/13/20150113091717604.pdf),
selected original teaching sections and word-specific publisher/dictionary entries.
Supported variants are not blanket errors. Exact indexed entries were usable
where a full reference fetch failed; a failed fetch was not called verification.
Twenty fresh 40-card author/editor pairs completed, with 62 editor findings resolved and all outputs read by EXECUTOR. One exhaustive final pass covered every field and mapping in consecutive 0001–0050, 0051–0100, 0101–0150, 0151–0250, 0251–0350, 0351–0450, 0451–0550, 0551–0650, 0651–0750, 0751–0800 partitions. Actual input capacity required smaller partitions; no completed card range was repeated. Verified final corrections were applied together before completion checks. The review reports source text and context, not audio, native-human review or attained proficiency.

Text checks cover source structure, mappings, boundaries, dependencies, duplicates
and logical audio paths. They do not certify naturalness, semantic novelty,
Simplified lexical identity or Pinyin. No MP3, audio review, runtime, application,
export or later-stage work is part of this source delivery.
